Its taking-as frame is crucial: attached {{ar:هَا}} ({{tr:hā}}) is the referent being treated, and {{ar:هُزُوًا}} ({{tr:huzuwan}}) is the status assigned to it. The feminine suffix can point to the path, signs, or revealed discourse in context, so contempt is broadened while anchored. Form VIII makes this deliberate reclassification, with the doubled consonant audibly tightening the act of taking, and the take-as-mockery formula appears in rejection scenes such as 6:10, 18:56, and 45:9.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:أ خ ذ}} ({{tr:ʾ-kh-dh}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"taking, adopting, and treating-as field, locally active as deliberate reclassification\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:يَتَّخِذَهَا}} ({{tr:yattakhidhahā}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"focus_occurrence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"31:6:6:2","source_type":"qac_morpheme","support_id":"sup_2d77e59b0a288814f33b","text":"{\"lemma_ar\":\"حَدِيث\",\"morph_features\":\"STEM|POS:N|LEM:Hadiyv|ROOT:Hdv|M|GEN\",\"morpheme_role\":\"STEM\",\"pos\":\"N\",\"qac_ref\":\"31:6:6:2\",\"qac_word_ref\":\"31:6:6\",\"root_ar\":\"ح د ث\",\"surface_ar\":\"حَدِيثِ\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"31:6:11","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_2fa51e63eab79c833cd6","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"singular construct path, the divine route from which deviation occurs\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:سَبِيلِ}} ({{tr:sabīli}}) names what is lost: not an undefined good, but a singular construct path belonging to God. The physical route image makes guidance concrete as a course from which a person can be diverted, while the construct with {{ar:ٱللَّهِ}} ({{tr:Allāhi}}) makes it a divine relation. The familiar formula {{ar:سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ}} ({{tr:sabīli llāhi}}) is used negatively here: the actor leads away from it. The word also specifies the guidance of 31:5 as route-guidance and prepares the contrast with following the path of one who turns to God (31:15).\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:س ب ل}} ({{tr:s-b-l}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"route, way, and flowing course imagery, narrowed locally to God’s established way\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:سَبِيلِ}} ({{tr:sabīli}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"31:6:2","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_3159a67debf3246c2ee9","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"partitive preposition extracting a subset from the human collective\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:مِنَ}} ({{tr:mina}}) makes the condemnation partitive: the ayah draws out a type from humanity rather than condemning humanity as a whole. The source sense still colors the figure as arising from the human collective, and the final vowel keeps the preposition flowing into its governed noun. The fronted phrase sets the social field before {{ar:مَن يَشْتَرِى}} ({{tr:man yashtarī}}) names the behavior.\",\"root_display\":\"\",\"root_gloss_range\":null,\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:مِنَ}} ({{tr:mina}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"31:6:14","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_32250f20f2a8f615f94f","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"genitive construct head of negation/otherness, forming a without-knowledge condition\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:غَيْرِ}} ({{tr:ghayri}}) is a noun of otherness, not a simple negative particle. Governed by {{ar:بِ}} ({{tr:bi}}) and governing {{ar:عِلْمٍ}} ({{tr:ʿilmin}}), it turns absence of knowledge into a compact nominal state. The broader change-root field is narrowed here to other-than or without; the payoff is that the actor stands outside knowledge while redirecting others from God’s path. The recurrence at 31:20 keeps this in Luqmān’s no-knowledge condemnation formula.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:غ ي ر}} ({{tr:gh-y-r}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"otherness and change field, narrowed locally to other-than or without, not alteration\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:غَيْرِ}} ({{tr:ghayri}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"31:6:7","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_332f6d9337b8f7743e38","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"definite genitive discourse noun subordinated to the diversion head\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:ٱلْحَدِيثِ}} ({{tr:al-ḥadīthi}}) is definite and genitive under {{ar:لَهْوَ}} ({{tr:lahwa}}), so the ayah targets a recognizable discourse field as subordinated to diversion. The root pressure of newness and reporting survives narrowly: novelty and speech are not condemned as such, but discourse becomes dangerous when made a commodity of distraction. This sets a contrast with the Quranic best-discourse formula at 39:23; 31:6 gives the anti-type, discourse commodified and later turned toward mockery.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:ح د ث}} ({{tr:ḥ-d-th}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"speech, report, occurrence, and newness field, narrowed here to discourse captured by diversion\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:ٱلْحَدِيثِ}} ({{tr:al-ḥadīthi}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"31:6:12:distribution","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_362d0289f3aee96d5e63","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"the ayah joins God’s path with action without knowledge\",\"reader_payoff\":\"The reader notices that the ayah joins God’s path with action without knowledge.\",\"reason\":\"The distribution CRITICAL rows support this payoff for {{ar:ٱللَّهِ}} ({{tr:Allāhi}}); guardrail evidence does not contradict it, and the prose preserves the local limit where needed.\",\"representative_source_ids\":[\"QI-dc543e9a\",\"MI-43479c3a\",\"QE-29c5956d\"],\"status\":\"used\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"31:6:22","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_365c1f91a98f5cfa54b2","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"indefinite nominative delayed subject: an unspecified punishment allocated after lahum\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:عَذَابٌ}} ({{tr:ʿadhābun}}) lands after fronted {{ar:لَهُمْ}} ({{tr:lahum}}), so allocation precedes the thing allocated. As an indefinite nominative, it leaves the exact punishment unspecified and open, while {{ar:مُّهِينٌ}} ({{tr:muhīnun}}) specifies its humiliating quality. The verbal-noun force keeps punishment from being a flat label; it is an allocated tormenting reality. The sweet-water contrast is narrowed background, adding deprivation pressure after guidance is traded for diversion. The paired tanwīn cadence makes punishment and humiliation land as one compact verdict. The formula {{ar:عَذَابٌ مُّهِينٌ}} ({{tr:ʿadhābun muhīnun}}) resolves the four-beat chain and recurs in punishment contexts, with same-surah echoes at 31:21 and 31:24.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:ع ذ ب}} ({{tr:ʿ-dh-b}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"punishment and torment field, with sweetness or freshness contrast kept as narrowed etymological pressure\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:عَذَابٌ}} ({{tr:ʿadhābun}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"31:6:5","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_36c6f8573154f027810a","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"imperfect Form VIII exchange verb: recurrent self-involving acquisition of diversion, with price left unstated\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:يَشْتَرِى}} ({{tr:yashtarī}}) makes the first concrete act commercial: the actor acquires {{ar:لَهْوَ}} ({{tr:lahwa}}), while the price is unstated. The imperfect and Form VIII make the purchase a recurring, self-involving pattern. The buy/sell root range is narrowed by the local object to purchasing diversion, but it still lets the reader feel exchange: guidance from the prior context is the value exposed to loss, as in the pattern of buying misguidance for guidance (2:16, 2:175). This purchase opens the chain that moves through misguidance, mockery, and punishment.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:ش ر ي}} ({{tr:sh-r-y}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"buy and sell exchange field, locally narrowed to purchasing while retaining moral-exchange pressure\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:يَشْتَرِى}} ({{tr:yashtarī}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"31:6:18","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_382f69ceea101c6fbe35","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"accusative gerund/result category: mockery imposed on the referent\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:هُزُوًا}} ({{tr:huzuwan}}) names the status the actor imposes. As an accusative gerund after {{ar:يَتَّخِذَهَا}} ({{tr:yattakhidhahā}}), it can be second object or state, but either way the referent is made into mockery rather than being mockery by nature. The root field keeps deliberate ridicule active while excluding reciprocal banter as the local focus. Qiraat hamza variation makes this word a sound-pressure point, while structurally it seals the action chain before {{ar:أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ}} ({{tr:ulāʾika}}) pivots to verdict. The internal reversal is exact: contempt is answered by humiliating punishment.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:ه ز أ}} ({{tr:h-z-ʾ}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"mockery, ridicule, and contempt field, locally active as object-status rather than reciprocal banter\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:هُزُوًا}} ({{tr:huzuwan}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"31:6:5:distribution","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_38ff7af260b9a4c121b4","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"the commerce verb recalls guidance-for-error exchange formulas (2:16\",\"reader_payoff\":\"The reader notices that the commerce verb recalls guidance-for-error exchange formulas (2:16, 2:175).\",\"reason\":\"The distribution CRITICAL rows support this payoff for {{ar:يَشْتَرِى}} ({{tr:yashtarī}}); The root field of dignity-stripping contempt answers the actor’s mockery, so the one who made the sacred referent an object of scorn meets degradation. Its final tanwīn reinforces the paired indefinite cadence with {{ar:عَذَابٌ}} ({{tr:ʿadhābun}}). The root image of being lost or hidden gives concrete force to the path phrase: the way is made to vanish from perception as one is led away. The geminated ending adds audible pressure to the causative act.
